**追加任务**：

1. **ckpt-80 也已可用** — 注册 `mvp2_eval_lr5e6_r1_ckpt80` 实验，eval 参数与 ckpt-60/ckpt-40 完全一致。ckpt-40 eval（cuda:2）完成后，立即在 cuda:2 启动 ckpt-80 eval。这样我们有完整 4 点轨迹：ckpt-20(0.0077) → 40 → 60 → 80。

2. **训练曲线深入分析**：step 86 的 f1_reward 仍然 0.005-0.016 非常令人担忧。请拉一下 step 20→40→60→80 的训练 log 中 f1_reward 和 ced_reward 的变化趋势，我需要判断是持续低位还是有明确上升趋势。

3. **SFT baseline（cuda:3 PID 104979）**：确认进度和预计完成时间。

优先级：先 submit ckpt-60 eval 占住 cuda:1，其余并行处理。

```json
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",
```

```text
1	"""DocRED 关系抽取评测模块。
2	
3	指标:
4	  - F1 / Ign-F1: 关系三元组级别 micro-F1，Ign-F1 排除训练集中出现的三元组
5	  - Evi-F1: 对正确预测的三元组，计算 evidence 句子级 F1
6	  - EDCR: Evidence Distractor Citation Rate，预测 evidence 中非 gold 的比例
7	
8	输入: 预测列表 + gold 标注列表（每个元素为文档级标注）
9	输出: 指标字典
10	"""
11	
12	import json
13	import logging
14	from collections import defaultdict
15	from typing import Optional
16	
17	logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
18	
19	
20	def _triple_key(doc_id: str, head: str, tail: str, relation: str) -> tuple:
21	    return (doc_id, head.lower().strip(), tail.lower().strip(), relation)
22	
23	
24	class DocREDEvaluator:
25	    """DocRED 评测器。
26	
27	    Args:
28	        train_triples: 训练集中的三元组集合，用于计算 Ign-F1。
29	                       每个元素为 (doc_title, head_name, tail_name, relation)。
30	                       为 None 时 Ign-F1 = F1。
31	    """
32	
33	    def __init__(self, train_triples: Optional[set[tuple]] = None):
34	        self.train_triples = train_triples or set()
35	
36	    @classmethod
37	    def from_train_file(cls, train_path: str) -> "DocREDEvaluator":
38	        """从训练集 JSON 文件构建 evaluator，自动提取训练集三元组。"""
39	        from freige.data.docred_processor import DOCRED_REL_INFO
40	
41	        with open(train_path) as f:
42	            train_data = json.load(f)
43	
44	        train_triples = set()
45	        for doc in train_data:
46	            doc_id = doc.get("title", "")
47	            vertex_set = doc["vertexSet"]
48	            for label in doc.get("labels", []):
49	                h_name = vertex_set[label["h"]][0]["name"]
50	                t_name = vertex_set[label["t"]][0]["name"]
```

The batch_eval command takes an experiment directory and likely orchestrates multiple evaluations across different checkpoints.


````text
```

So batch_eval uses `--sft_adapter`. But the single-checkpoint inference might use `--model_path` for the GRPO checkpoint and `--base_model` for Qwen3-4B, and maybe the SFT adapter is automatically loaded because the GRPO checkpoint is on top of it.

Actually wait — in the SFT baseline eval, `--model_path /root/autodl-tmp/sft_output` points to the SFT adapter, and `--base_model /root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3-4B` is the base. For GRPO ckpt eval, the ckpt is a LoRA adapter trained on top of SFT+base

The inference module probably loads the base model first, then applies the SFT adapter if provided, then stacks the GRPO adapter on top—or the GRPO checkpoint might already have both merged together. Since I can't check the inference code locally and the ckpt-20 eval script is on the remote server, I'll go with the safest approach: replicate the same command pattern that worked for ckpt-20, just swapping in the new checkpoint number. at the batch_eval, it needs `--sft_adapter`. python -m freige.eval.inference --model_path /root/autodl-tmp/grpo_ced_lr5e6/checkpoint-60 --base_model /root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3-4B --data_path /root/autodl-tmp/data/docred --split dev --batch_size 16 --max_new_tokens 1024 --seed 42 --output_dir /root/autodl-tmp/eval_results/grpo_ced_lr5e6_ckpt60 --quantize
```

Wait, I need to think through this more carefully. The SFT eval uses `--model_path` pointing to the SFT adapter, but for GRPO the checkpoint is trained on top of that SFT base. Since GRPO training in TRL/PEFT typically only saves the adapter delta, loading just the GRPO checkpoint would miss the SFT weights underneath. The inference module probably needs to load both adapters in sequence, so I should check the batch_eval source code to see how it actually handles this.
````

```text
1	"""批量评估: 遍历 {实验}×{checkpoint} 组合，调用 inference.py 并汇总 metrics。
2	
3	用法:
4	    python -m freige.eval.batch_eval \
5	        --experiments /root/autodl-tmp/grpo_ced_r5,/root/autodl-tmp/grpo_extract_r3 \
6	        --base_model /root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3-4B \
7	        --sft_adapter /root/autodl-tmp/sft_output \
8	        --data_path /root/autodl-tmp/data/docred \
9	        --output_dir /root/autodl-tmp/eval_results/batch \
10	        --checkpoints best,last
11	"""
12	
13	import argparse
14	import csv
15	import json
16	import subprocess
17	import sys
18	from pathlib import Path
19	
20	from freige.eval.find_best_checkpoint import find_best_checkpoint
21	
22	
23	SUMMARY_METRICS = ["f1", "ign_f1", "evi_f1", "edcr", "format_ok_rate", "n_predictions", "n_truncated"]
24	
25	
26	def discover_checkpoints(exp_dir: Path) -> list[Path]:
27	    dirs = sorted(exp_dir.glob("checkpoint-*"), key=lambda p: int(p.name.split("-")[-1]))
28	    return dirs
29	
30	
31	def resolve_checkpoints(exp_dir: Path, mode: str) -> list[Path]:
32	    """根据 --checkpoints 参数解析要评估的 checkpoint 列表。"""
33	    all_ckpts = discover_checkpoints(exp_dir)
34	    if not all_ckpts:
35	        print(f"WARNING: no checkpoints in {exp_dir}", file=sys.stderr)
36	        return []
37	
38	    if mode == "all":
39	        return all_ckpts
40	
41	    tokens = [t.strip() for t in mode.split(",")]
42	    result = []
43	    seen = set()
44	
45	    for tok in tokens:
46	        if tok == "last":
47	            path = all_ckpts[-1]
48	            if path not in seen:
49	                result.append(path)
50	                seen.add(path)
51	        elif tok == "best":
52	            info = find_best_checkpoint(str(exp_dir))
53	            if info:
54	                path = Path(info["path"])
55	                if path not in seen:
56	                    result.append(path)
57	                    seen.add(path)
58	            else:
59	                print(f"WARNING: could not find best checkpoint for {exp_dir}", file=sys.stderr)
60	        else:
61	            path = exp_dir / f"checkpoint-{tok}"
62	            if path.is_dir():
63	                if path not in seen:
64	                    result.append(path)
65	                    seen.add(path)
66	            else:
67	                print(f"WARNING: {path} does not exist, skipping", file=sys.stderr)
68	
69	    return result
70	
71	
72	def run_single_eval(
73	    checkpoint_path: Path,
74	    base_model: str,
75	    sft_adapter: str | None,
76	    data_path: str,
77	    split: str,
78	    output_dir: Path,
79	    batch_size: int,
80	) -> dict | None:
```

````text
    --split &lt;split> \
    --output_dir &lt;output_dir> \
    --batch_size &lt;batch_size> \
    [--sft_adapter &lt;sft_adapter>]

I need to submit this right away while also queuing up checkpoint-80 as a pending experiment, spawning a worker to analyze the training curves across different checkpoints, and checking on the SFT baseline—though I should remember that the SFT baseline doesn't need the adapter flag since the model path itself is the fine-tuned model, whereas GRPO checkpoints require the adapter because they're built on top of the SFT output.

I'm prioritizing submitting checkpoint-60's eval first, then handling the registry update for checkpoint-80, spawning workers for the training curve analysis and SFT baseline status check in parallel.   "task_prompt": "## 任务：诊断 ckpt-60 eval 失败并重新提交\n\n### 背景\n实验 `mvp2_eval_lr5e6_r1_ckpt60` 在 cuda:1 上提交后 11 秒内失败（EXIT_CODE=1）。\n\n失败命令：\n```\nbash -c \"source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cd /root/autodl-tmp/freige && python -m freige.eval.batch_eval --exp_dir /root/autodl-tmp/grpo_ced_lr5e6 --sft_adapter /root/autodl-tmp/sft_output --output_dir /root/autodl-tmp/eval_results/lr5e6_r1_ckpt60 --checkpoints 60 --quantize\"\n```\n\n### 步骤\n1. **SSH 到 rtx5090-4x**，检查失败日志：\n   - `cat ~/runs/faith-evidence-ie/mvp2_eval_lr5e6_r1_ckpt60.log | tail -50`\n   - 检查 `/root/autodl-tmp/eval_results/lr5e6_r1_ckpt60/` 目录下是否有 error.log\n   - 确认 `/root/autodl-tmp/grpo_ced_lr5e6/checkpoint-60` 存在\n\n2. **诊断失败原因**：可能是 `--quantize` 不在 batch_eval 的参数中、import 错误、路径问题等。\n\n3. **修复并重新提交**：用 `submit_training_job` 提交修复后的命令到 cuda:1。\n   - 服务器：rtx5090-4x\n   - GPU：cuda:1（已空闲）\n   - 使用 conda 环境：`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`\n   - 推荐用单 checkpoint inference 替代 batch_eval：\n     ```\n     python -m freige.eval.inference --model_path /root/autodl-tmp/grpo_ced_lr5e6/checkpoint-60 --base_model /root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3-4B --sft_adapter /root/autodl-tmp/sft_output --data_path /root/autodl-tmp/data/docred --split dev --batch_size 16 --max_new_tokens 1024 --seed 42 --output_dir /root/autodl-tmp/eval_results/grpo_ced_lr5e6_ckpt60 --quantize\n     ```\n   - 注意：先确认 freige.eval.inference 模块的参数列表（`python -m freige.eval.inference --help`）\n\n4. **更新 registry**：\n   - 先 `update_registry(exp_id=\"mvp2_eval_lr5e6_r1_ckpt60\", field=\"status\", value=\"ALLOCATED\")` 重置状态\n   - 然后用 `submit_training_job` 提交\n\n### 实验元数据\n- exp_id: mvp2_eval_lr5e6_r1_ckpt60\n- parent: mvp2_grpo_ced_lr5e6_r1\n- 数据: /root/autodl-tmp/data/docred/dev.json (985 docs)\n- checkpoint: /root/autodl-tmp/grpo_ced_lr5e6/checkpoint-60\n- 输出: /root/autodl-tmp/eval_results/grpo_ced_lr5e6_ckpt60\n\n### 产出\n完成后报告：失败原因、修复方案、新 PID。",
````

````json
  "project": "faith-evidence-ie",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：拉取 GRPO lr5e6 训练曲线（step 20→40→60→80）\n\n### 背景\n实验 `mvp2_grpo_ced_lr5e6_r1` 在 rtx5090-4x cuda:0 上运行（PID 90080），当前 step 84/200。\nDirector 需要 step 20/40/60/80 四个点的 f1_reward 和 ced_reward 趋势，判断是持续低位还是有上升趋势。\n\n已知信息（来自 registry notes）：\n- f1_reward 波动大 (0→0.042)\n- format_reward 稳步上升 0.138→0.249\n- total_reward 0.138-0.318 区间, step 40 最优 (0.318), step 80 回升 (0.283)\n- KL 二次飙升 step 65=10.3 但 step 70 立即恢复到 0.012\n\n但这不够细致。Director 需要精确数据。\n\n### 步骤（全部用 ssh_read_only）\n1. SSH 到 rtx5090-4x\n\n2. 读取训练日志，提取 step 15-86 的所有 reward 指标：\n   ```\n   cat ~/runs/faith-evidence-ie/mvp2_grpo_ced_lr5e6_r1.log | grep -E \"(f1_reward|ced_reward|format_reward|total_reward|kl)\" | head -100\n   ```\n\n3. 如果日志中不够详细，尝试从 W&B local 目录或 tmux session 读取：\n   ```\n   tmux capture-pane -t mvp2_grpo_ced_lr5e6_r1 -p -S -200 2>/dev/null | grep -E \"(reward|step|kl)\" | tail -50\n   ```\n\n4. 也检查 wandb offline 目录（如果有）：\n   ```\n   ls /root/autodl-tmp/wandb/ 2>/dev/null | tail -5\n   find /root/autodl-tmp/wandb/ -name \"*.wandb\" -newer /root/autodl-tmp/grpo_ced_lr5e6/checkpoint-20 2>/dev/null | head -5\n   ```\n\n5. 如果上面不够，尝试直接用 W&B API（可能 wandb 环境变量已在 conda env 中配置）：\n   ```\n   source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && python -c \"\n   import wandb\n   api = wandb.Api()\n   # 尝试列出 runs\n   runs = api.runs('faith-evidence-ie', filters={'display_name': 'grpo-s3-ced-lr5e6-r1'})\n   for r in runs:\n       hist = r.scan_history(keys=['f1_reward', 'ced_reward', 'format_reward', 'total_reward', 'kl', '_step'])\n       for row in hist:\n           step = row.get('_step', '?')\n           if step in [20,40,60,80] or (isinstance(step, int) and step % 10 == 0):\n               print(f'step={step} f1_r={row.get(\\\"f1_reward\\\",\\\"?\\\")} ced_r={row.get(\\\"ced_reward\\\",\\\"?\\\")} fmt_r={row.get(\\\"format_reward\\\",\\\"?\\\")} total_r={row.get(\\\"total_reward\\\",\\\"?\\\")} kl={row.get(\\\"kl\\\",\\\"?\\\")}')\n   \" 2>&1 | head -30\n   ```\n   注意：这个用 ssh_read_only 执行（不改变服务器状态）\n\n### 产出\n报告一个表格：\n\n| Step | f1_reward | ced_reward | format_reward | total_reward | kl |\n|------|-----------|------------|--------------|--------------|-----|\n\n重点关注 step 20, 40, 60, 80 四个 checkpoint 点。如果有中间步骤数据也一并列出，帮助判断趋势。\n\n额外回答：f1_reward 是持续低位还是有明确上升趋势？ced_reward 变化情况？",
````
